## the bottom table of context and other events need to be adjusted to Pax - Lexus
## i left one event as example for the rest of the events, if you're not sure check out KR code

add_namespace = annex

##   C E N T R A L   E U R O P E   ##

country_event = { #Fate of France
	id = annex.1001
	title = annexation_16
	desc = annexation_16_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							state = 17 #Meurthe
							state = 29 #Pas-de-Calais
							state = 31 #Roussillon
							state = 685 #Champagne Ardenne
							state = 735 #Savoy
							state = 809 #Franche Comte
							state = 855 #Dunkerque Corridor
							state = 1206 #Moselle
							state = 1207 #Verdun
						}
					} 
					remove_core_of = FRA
					retain_state = yes
				}
				remove_claim_by = FRA
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet (Almost) Entire France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.1001.a"
		name = annex.1001.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_france = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FRA }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = FRA }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #France Delenda Est
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.1001.b"
		name = annex.1001.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_france = yes
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
		custom_effect_tooltip = annexations_country_to_be_divided_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					is_core_of = FRA
					NOT = {
						state = 16 #Paris
						state = 24 #Centre
						state = 785 #Picardy
					}
				}
				remove_core_of = FRA
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = FRA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Monarchy_Split
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			FRA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_Occupied_Republic_Split
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		FRA = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = FRA }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1001 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Occitania
	id = annex.1002
	title = annexation_1202
	desc = annexation_1202_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = {
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Occitania
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.1002.a"
		name = annex.1002.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = OCT }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes
		}
		release_targeted_tag = yes
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = { is_occitania = yes }
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1002 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Burgundy
	id = annex.1003
	title = annexation_27
	desc = annexation_27_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = {
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			FRA = { save_event_target_as = france }
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Burgundy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.1003.a"
		name = annex.1003.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BRG }
		18 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Champagne
		27 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Bourgogne
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			BRG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BRG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			BRG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BRG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_France_Delenda_Est
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		18 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Champagne
		27 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Bourgogne
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		18 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Champagne
		27 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #Bourgogne
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		18 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Champagne
		27 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Bourgogne
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1003 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Loire
	id = annex.1006
	title = annexation_30
	desc = annexation_30_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					14 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Brittany
					24 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Centre
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				24 = { #Centre
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:24.owner = { save_event_target_as = france }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				14 = { #Brittany
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:france }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:14.owner = { save_event_target_as = brittany }
		}
	}

	option = { #give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.europe.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 90
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_france = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				is_ally_with = event_target:brittany
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:france = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		30 = { #Loire
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes 
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #give to Brittany
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.1006.a"
		name = annex.1006.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = brittany
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:brittany }
				is_ally_with = event_target:france
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:brittany = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:brittany }
		30 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Loire
	}

	option = { #give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		30 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Loire
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		30 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Loire
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1006 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}


country_event = { #Fate of Calais
	id = annex.1007
	title = annexation_29
	desc = annexation_29_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					785 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Picardy
					855 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Dunkerque
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				785 = { #Picardy
					owner = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes }
				}
			}
			var:785.owner = { save_event_target_as = picardy }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				855 = { #Dunkerque
					annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes
					owner = {
						NOT = { tag = event_target:picardy }
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
			}
			var:855.owner = { save_event_target_as = dunkerque }
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to owner of Picardy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1007 option annex.1007.a"
		name = annex.1007.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = picardy
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:picardy }
				is_ally_with = event_target:dunkerque
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = dunkerque }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:picardy = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:picardy }
		29 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Dunkerque
	}
	option = { #Give to owner of Dunkerque
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1007 option annex.1007.b"
		name = annex.1007.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = dunkerque
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:dunkerque }
				is_ally_with = event_target:picardy
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = picardy }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:dunkerque = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:dunkerque }

				factor = 0

country_event = { #Fate of Britain
	id = annex.1013
	title = annexation_126
	desc = annexation_126_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_great_britain = no
					} 
					remove_core_of = ENG
					retain_state = yes
				}
				remove_claim_by = ENG
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.1013.a"
		name = annex.1013.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_britain = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ENG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Split Up Great Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.1013.b"
		name = annex.1013.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}

		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				annexations_AI_country_wants_to_dismantle_britain = yes
			}
		}

		custom_effect_tooltip = annexations_country_to_be_divided_tt

		set_global_flag = PB_ENG_Divided_via_Peace


		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ENG }

		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_england = no
					} 
					remove_core_of = ENG
				}
				remove_claim_by = ENG
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = ENG }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ENG_puppet_tooltip
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = ENG }
			release_targeted_tag = yes
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Monarchy_Split
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			ENG = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ENG_Occupied_Republic_Split
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		ENG = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Annex
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1013 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = { is_core_of = ENG }
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
	}

	option = { #Cancel

		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Portugal
	id = annex.1016
	title = annexation_112
	desc = annexation_112_desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_Generic_Art_Gallery

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = POR } }
				POR = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { country_exists = PEF } }
				PEF = { clear_cores_of_tag_if_possible = yes }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = POR
						original_tag = PEF
						original_tag = SPR
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			random_other_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = POR
						original_tag = PEF
					}
					is_ally_with = ROOT
					OR = {
						is_subject = no
						is_subject_of = ROOT
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = portugal
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = portugal } }
				if = {
					limit = { POR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					POR = { save_event_target_as = portugal }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { PEF = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
					PEF = { save_event_target_as = portugal }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_event_target = spain } }
				if = {
					limit = { SPR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }

			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PEF }
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_portugal = yes
				NOT = { is_core_of = var:tag_to_release }

			add_claim_by = var:tag_to_release
		}
		release_targeted_tag_with_claims = yes
	}

			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SPR }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SPR = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SPR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SPR = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SPR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1018 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		SPA = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1018 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Parma
	id = annex.1032
	title = annexation_834
	desc = annexation_834_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_italy = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_italian_tag = yes }
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			annexations_create_italian_targets = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:162.owner = { #Tuscany
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				var:162.owner = { save_event_target_as = tuscany }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:158.owner = { #Piedmont
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				var:158.owner = { save_event_target_as = piedmont }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:159.owner = { #Lombardy
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
					}
				}
				var:159.owner = { save_event_target_as = lombardy }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Parma
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1032 option annex.1032.a"
		name = annex.1032.a
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_italian_splinters = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PRM }
		834 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Parma
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			PRM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			PRM = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = PRM_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

	}

	option = { #Puppet Emilia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1032 option annex.1032.b"
		name = annex.1032.b
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_release_italian_splinters = yes
			835 = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT 
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PRM }
		834 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Parma
		835 = { #Modena
			remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes 
			transfer_if_owned = yes
		}

		}

			}

			}
		}

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_event_target = italy
				has_event_target = italy_soc
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}

				factor = 0
				NOT = {

					}
				}
			}
		}

	}

}

country_event = { #Fate of the San Marino
	id = annex.1038
	title = annexation_970
	desc = annexation_970_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			annexations_reset_italy = yes

				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			annexations_create_italian_targets = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					var:157.owner = { #Marche
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
						original_tag = PAP
					}
				}
				var:157.owner = { save_event_target_as = papacy }
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet San Marino

		name = annex.1038.a
		trigger = {

		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PAP }
		970 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #San Marino
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SNM = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PAP }

		970 = { transfer_if_owned = yes } #San Marino

			}
		}

			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy 
						is_ally_with = event_target:italy_soc
					}
				}
			}
		}
		annexations_give_italy_to_target = yes

	}

	option = { #Give it to Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1053 option annex.1053.b"
		name = annex.1053.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ITA = {
						exists = yes
					}
					IST = {
						exists = no
					}
				}
				AND = {
					ITA = {
						exists = no
					}
					IST = {
						exists = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { base = 0 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				ITA = {
					exists = yes
				}
				IST = {
					exists = no
				}
			}
			ITA = {
				transfer_state = 39
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				IST = {
					exists = yes
				}
				ITA = {
					exists = no
				}
			}
			IST = {
				transfer_state = 39
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Return to Austria
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1053 option annex.1051.c"
		name = annex.1053.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			AUS = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		AUS = {
			transfer_state = 39
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1053 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1053 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = NWL
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1053 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

	title = annexation_sweden
	desc = annexation_sweden_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		138 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1011 = { retain_state = yes } 
		144 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1363 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1362 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1366 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1020 = { retain_state = yes } 
		666 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1019 = { retain_state = yes } 
		1422 = { retain_state = yes } 
	}

	option = { #Release Sweden
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1062 option annex.1062.a"
		name = annex.1062.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SWE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SWE = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SWE_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SWE }
			release_targeted_tag = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1062 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = NWL
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1062 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Banat
	id = annex.1082
	title = annexation_banat
	desc = annexation_banat_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		764 = { retain_state = yes }
	}

	option = { #Release Banat
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1082 option annex.1082.a"
		name = annex.1082.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = BNA }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					NOT = {
						original_tag = GER
					}
				}
			}
			BNA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BNA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = {
					has_monarchist_government = yes
					original_tag = GER
				}
			}
			BNA = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = BNA_Occupied_Monarchy_Kraut
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			BNA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = BNA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give the land to Hungary
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1082 option annex.1082.a"
		name = annex.1082.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			HUN = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		HUN = {
			transfer_state = 82
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1082 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1082 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = NWL
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1082 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

	id = annex.1090
	title = annexation_vardar
	desc = annexation_vardar_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Release Macedonia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1090 option annex.1090.a"
		name = annex.1090.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = MAC }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			MAC = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAC_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}


		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			MAC = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = MAC_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Transfer to Bulgaria
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1090 option annex.1090.b"
		name = annex.1090.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			BUL = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		BUL = {
			transfer_state = 106
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1090 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1090 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = NWL
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1090 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Sardinia
	id = annex.1099
	title = annexation_sardinia
	desc = annexation_sardinia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				ITA = {
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			ITA = { save_event_target_as = italy }
		}
	}

	option = { #Release Sardinia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.1099.a"
		name = annex.1099.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = SRD }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			SRD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SRD_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			SRD = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = SRD_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give Sardinia to Italy
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.1099.b"
		name = annex.1099.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = PB_GLOBAL_Splitaly
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { base = 0 }

		ITA = {
			transfer_state = 114
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1099 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}

country_event = { #Fate of Galicia
	id = annex.1101
	title = annexation_polish_galicia
	desc = annexation_polish_galicia_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		91 = { retain_state = yes } #Lwow
		1402 = { retain_state = yes } #Tarnopol
		89 = { retain_state = yes } #Stanislawow
		80 = { retain_state = yes } #Bucovina
		1036 = { retain_state = yes } #Southern Bucovina
	}

	option = { #Release Galicia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1101 option annex.1101.a"
		name = annex.1101.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = PGA }
			custom_effect_tooltip = FRA_puppet_tooltip
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = PGA }
			release_targeted_tag = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			PGA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PGA_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			PGA = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = PGA_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
	
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1101 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				#is_starting_french_state = yes
				is_allowed_annexation_state = yes
			}
			transfer_state_and_notify = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1101 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = NWL
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		every_owned_state = {
			add_claim_of_ROOT = yes
		}
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1101 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

						original_tag = GER
					}
				}
			}
			VGE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VGE_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			VGE = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = VGE_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Give it back to Russia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.1123.b"
		name = annex.1123.b
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			RUS = {
				exists = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		RUS = {
			transfer_state = 401
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Integrate it
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.1123.c"
		name = annex.1123.c
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
			OR = {
				original_tag = GER
				original_tag = PRS
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 10
		}

		ROOT = {
			add_state_core = 401
		}

		recheck_annexations = yes
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1123 option annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #Fate of Doggerland
	id = annex.1135
	title = annexation_1421
	desc = annexation_1421_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				remove_core_of = CYP
				remove_claim_by = CYP
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = ENG
						original_tag = HOL
					}
				}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ENG = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				ENG = {
					save_event_target_as = britain
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { HOL = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				HOL = {
					save_event_target_as = netherlands
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { FRA = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				FRA = {
					save_event_target_as = france
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Doggerland
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1135 option annex.1135.a"
		name = annex.1135.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				NOT = {
					original_tag = ENG
					original_tag = HOL
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = -2
				annexations_can_annex = yes
				OR = {
					original_tag = ENG
					original_tag = HOL
				}
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = DGG }
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes original_tag = ENG } }
			DGG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DGG_League_Mandate
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes NOT = { original_tag = ENG } } }
			DGG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DGG_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			DGG = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = DGG_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1135 option annex.europe.give_to_britain"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_britain
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = britain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
				126 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #London
				7 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #Holland
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				event_target:britain = { is_ally_of_ROOT = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:britain }
		DGG = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to Netherlands
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1135 option annex.europe.give_to_netherlands"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_netherlands
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = netherlands
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:netherlands }
				126 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #London
				7 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #Holland
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					is_ally_with = event_target:france
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				event_target:netherlands = { is_ally_of_ROOT = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:netherlands }
		DGG = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to France
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1135 option annex.europe.give_to_france"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_france
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = france
			is_in_faction_with = FRA
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:france }
				29 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #Pas-de-Calais
				16 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = no } #Paris
				OR = {
					is_major = yes
					OR = {
						is_ally_with = event_target:britain
						is_ally_with = event_target:netherlands
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				event_target:france = { is_ally_of_ROOT = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:france }
		DGG = { set_transfer_target_cores = yes }
		transfer_targeted_territory = yes
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord

country_event = { #Fate of Cyprus
	id = annex.1142
	title = annexation_183
	desc = annexation_183_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			every_state = {
				remove_core_of = CYP
				remove_claim_by = CYP
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						original_tag = TUR
						original_tag = GRE
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = can_annex
			}
			if = {
				limit = { GRE = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				GRE = {
					save_event_target_as = greece
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { TUR = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				TUR = {
					save_event_target_as = turkey
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ENG = { ROOT_can_grant_land = yes } }
				ENG = {
					save_event_target_as = britain
					add_to_array = { ROOT.potential_grant_targets = THIS }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Puppet Cyprus
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1142 option annex.1142.a"
		name = annex.1142.a
		trigger = {
			is_subject = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = greece
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:greece
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = turkey
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:turkey
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_event_target = britain
				OR = {
					is_major = no
					is_ally_with = event_target:britain
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = CYP }
		183 = { remove_claim_and_add_core_of_target = yes } #Cyprus
		release_targeted_tag = yes

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			CYP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = CYP_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
	
		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_republican_government = yes } }
			CYP = {
				drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
				set_cosmetic_tag = CYP_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to Greece
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1142 option annex.europe.give_to_greece"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_greece
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = greece
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = GRE }
		183 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Cyprus
	}

	option = { #Give to Turkey
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1142 option annex.europe.give_to_turkey"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_turkey
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = turkey
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_ally_with = GRE
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = TUR }
		183 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Cyprus
	}

	option = { #Give to Britain
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1142 option annex.europe.give_to_britain"
		name = annex.europe.give_to_britain
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = britain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 20

			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:britain }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:britain = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:britain }
		183 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Cyprus

			ADR = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ADR_Occupied_Republic
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { has_monarchist_government = yes } }
			ADR = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = ADR_Occupied_Monarchy
			}
		}
	}

	option = { #Give to owner of Roussillion
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1150 option annex.1150.b"
		name = annex.1150.b
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = roussillon
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:roussillon }
				is_ally_with = event_target:catalonia
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = catalonia }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:roussillon = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:roussillon }
		913 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Andorra
	}

	option = { #Give to Catalonia
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1150 option annex.1150.c"
		name = annex.1150.c
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = catalonia
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 40
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:catalonia }
				is_ally_with = event_target:roussillon
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { has_event_target = roussillon }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:catalonia = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:catalonia }
		913 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Andorra
	}

	option = { #Give to overlord

country_event = { #Fate of Channel Islands
	id = annex.1151
	title = annexation_973
	desc = annexation_973_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						127 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #London
						original_tag = ENG
					}
					15 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Normandy
					annexations_AI_wants_to_own_channel_islands = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = can_annex
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ENG = {
					AND = {
						ROOT_can_grant_land = yes 
						owns_state = 127 #London
					}
				}
			}
			ENG = { save_event_target_as = britain }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:14.owner = { #Brittany
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			var:14.owner = { save_event_target_as = brittany }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				var:15.owner = { #Normandy
					ROOT_can_grant_land = yes
				}
			}
			var:15.owner = { save_event_target_as = normandy }
		}
	}

			has_event_target = britain
		}

			base = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_AI_wants_to_annex = yes
			}

				factor = 0
				#annexations_AI_will_consider_returning_colonies = no

				factor = 0

				factor = 0.1

		973 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Channel Islands
	}
	option = { #Give to Normandy

		name = annex.1151.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = normandy
		}
		ai_chance = {

	option = { #Give to Basque
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1155 option annex.1155.a"
		name = annex.1155.a
		trigger = {
			has_event_target = spain
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				annexations_can_annex = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { is_ally_with = event_target:basque }
				is_ally_with = event_target:spain
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				event_target:basque = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = event_target:basque }
		1194 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Cantabria
	}
	option = { #Give to overlord
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1155 option annex.give_to_overlord"
		name = annex.give_to_overlord
		trigger = {
			annexations_should_give_lands_to_overlord = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { tag_to_release = overlord }
		1194 = { transfer_state_and_notify = yes } #Cantabria
	}

	option = { #Integrate
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1155 option annex.integration"
		name = annex.integration
		trigger = {
			annexations_can_annex = yes
		}
		1194 = { add_claim_of_ROOT = yes } #Cantabria
	}

	option = { #Cancel
		log = "[GetLogInfo]: event annex.1155 option name = annex.cancel"
		name = annex.cancel
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}

	after = {
		recheck_annexations = yes
	}
}

	id = annex.1156
	title = annexation_701
	desc = annexation_701_desc
	picture = GFX_report_PB_Peace_Deals_Europe

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						177 = { is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes } #Ibiza & Mallorca
						annexations_major_can_annex_strategic_territories = yes
					}
				}

			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					177 = { #Ibiza & Mallorca
						annexations_state_owner_is_valid_grant_target = yes

		}
